READER.src_delta_value\n  FROM type_reader READER\n  \n)\n\n, type_target AS(\n  SELECT\n    TGT.ORDER_key AS tgt_focal_key,\n    TGT.eff_tmstp AS tgt_eff_tmstp,\n    TGT.type_key AS tgt_type_key,\n    TGT.seq_nbr AS tgt_seq_nbr,\n    TGT.row_st AS tgt_row_st,\n    TGT.sta_tmstp AS tgt_sta_tmstp,\n    TGT.end_tmstp AS tgt_end_tmstp,\n    TGT.val_str AS tgt_val_str,\n    TGT.val_num AS tgt_val_num,\n    TGT.uom AS tgt_uom,\n    TGT.inst_row_key AS tgt_inst_row_key,\n    CASE WHEN COALESCE(\n        CAST(sta_tmstp AS VARCHAR),\n        CAST(end_tmstp AS VARCHAR),\n        val_str,\n        CAST(val_num AS VARCHAR),\n        uom\n      ) IS NULL THEN NULL\n      ELSE md5(CONCAT(COALESCE(CAST(seq_nbr AS VARCHAR),'¤¤NULL¤¤'),COALESCE(CAST(sta_tmstp AS VARCHAR),'¤¤NULL¤¤'),COALESCE(CAST(end_tmstp AS VARCHAR),'¤¤NULL¤¤'),COALESCE(CAST(val_str AS VARCHAR),'¤¤NULL¤¤'),COALESCE(CAST(val_num AS VARCHAR),'¤¤NULL¤¤'),COALESCE(CAST(uom AS VARCHAR),'¤¤NULL¤¤')))\n    END AS tgt_delta_value\n  FROM (\n    SELECT DISTINCT\n      TGT.ORDER_key,\n      TGT.eff_tmstp,\n      TGT.type_key,\n      TGT.seq_nbr,\n      TGT.row_st,\n      TGT.sta_tmstp,\n      TGT.end_tmstp,\n      TGT.val_str,\n      TGT.val_num,\n      TGT.uom,\n      TGT.inst_row_key,\n      RANK() OVER (\n        PARTITION BY TGT.ORDER_key, TGT.type_key, TGT.eff_tmstp\n        ORDER BY TGT.ver_tmstp DESC\n      ) AS RNK\n    FROM dab.ORDER_desc TGT\n    WHERE TGT.type_key = CAST(17 AS BIGINT)\n      AND TGT.eff_tmstp <= COALESCE(CAST(LOCALTIMESTAMP AS TIMESTAMP), LOCALTIMESTAMP)\n      AND TGT.data_key = CAST(3272468793414818894 AS BIGINT)\n      AND TGT.inst_row_key = CAST(6758675485452523046 AS BIGINT)\n  ) TGT\n  WHERE TGT.RNK = 1 AND row_st = 'Y'\n)\n, type_delta AS(\n  SELECT\n    SRC.src_focal_key,\n    SRC.src_type_key,\n    SRC.src_seq_nbr,\n    COALESCE(SRC.eff_tmstp, LOCALTIMESTAMP) AS eff_tmstp,\n    COALESCE(SRC.ver_tmstp, LOCALTIMESTAMP) AS ver_tmstp,\n    SRC.src_sta_tmstp,\n    SRC.src_end_tmstp,\n    SRC.src_val_str,\n    SRC.src_val_num,\n    SRC.src_uom,\n    SRC.src_inst_row_key,\n    TGT.tgt_focal_key,\n    TGT.tgt_eff_tmstp,\n    TGT.tgt_type_key,\n    TGT.tgt_seq_nbr,\n    TGT.tgt_sta_tmstp,\n    TGT.tgt_end_tmstp,\n    TGT.tgt_val_str,\n    TGT.tgt_val_num,\n    TGT.tgt_uom,\n    TGT.tgt_inst_row_key,\n    CASE\n      WHEN tgt_focal_key IS NULL THEN 'NEW'\n      WHEN src_focal_key IS NULL THEN 'DEL'\n      WHEN (src_delta_value = tgt_delta_value\n            OR (src_delta_value IS NULL AND tgt_delta_value IS NULL)) THEN 'NOCHANGE'\n      WHEN (src_delta_value IS NULL AND tgt_delta_value IS NOT NULL) THEN 'DEL'\n      WHEN (src_delta_value <> tgt_delta_value) THEN 'CHANGE'\n      ELSE 'CHANGE'\n    END AS deltaflag\n  FROM type_integration SRC\n  FULL OUTER JOIN type_target TGT\n    ON SRC.src_focal_key = TGT.tgt_focal_key\n    AND SRC.eff_tmstp = TGT.tgt_eff_tmstp\n    AND SRC.src_type_key = TGT.tgt_type_key\n    AND SRC.src_seq_nbr = TGT.tgt_seq_nbr\n)\n, type_load AS(\n  SELECT\n    CASE WHEN (deltaflag = 'CHANGE' OR deltaflag = 'NEW') THEN src_focal_key\n         WHEN deltaflag = 'DEL' THEN tgt_focal_key\n         ELSE src_focal_key END AS focal_key,\n    CASE WHEN (deltaflag = 'CHANGE' OR deltaflag = 'NEW') THEN src_type_key\n         WHEN deltaflag = 'DEL' THEN tgt_type_key\n         ELSE src_type_key END AS type_key,\n    CASE WHEN (deltaflag = 'CHANGE' OR deltaflag = 'NEW') THEN src_seq_nbr\n         WHEN deltaflag = 'DEL' THEN tgt_seq_nbr\n         ELSE src_seq_nbr END AS seq_nbr,\n    CASE WHEN (deltaflag = 'CHANGE' OR deltaflag = 'NEW') THEN eff_tmstp\n         WHEN deltaflag = 'DEL' THEN tgt_eff_tmstp\n         ELSE eff_tmstp END AS eff_tmstp,\n    ver_tmstp,\n    CASE WHEN (deltaflag = 'CHANGE' OR deltaflag = 'NEW') THEN 'Y'\n         WHEN deltaflag = 'DEL' THEN 'N'\n         ELSE 'Y' END AS row_st,\n    CASE WHEN (deltaflag = 'CHANGE' OR deltaflag = 'NEW') THEN src_sta_tmstp\n         WHEN deltaflag = 'DEL' THEN tgt_sta_tmstp\n         ELSE src_sta_tmstp END AS sta_tmstp,\n    CASE WHEN (deltaflag = 'CHANGE' OR deltaflag = 'NEW') THEN src_end_tmstp\n         WHEN deltaflag = 'DEL' THEN tgt_end_tmstp\n         ELSE src_end_tmstp END AS end_tmstp,\n    CASE WHEN (deltaflag = 'CHANGE' OR deltaflag = 'NEW') THEN src_val_str\n         WHEN deltaflag = 'DEL' THEN tgt_val_str\n         ELSE src_val_str END AS val_str,\n    CASE WHEN (deltaflag = 'CHANGE' OR deltaflag = 'NEW') THEN src_val_num\n         WHEN deltaflag = 'DEL' THEN tgt_val_num\n         ELSE src_val_num END AS val_num,\n    CASE WHEN (deltaflag = 'CHANGE' OR deltaflag = 'NEW') THEN src_uom\n         WHEN deltaflag = 'DEL' THEN tgt_uom\n         ELSE src_uom END AS uom,\n    CASE WHEN (deltaflag = 'CHANGE' OR deltaflag = 'NEW') THEN src_inst_row_key\n         WHEN deltaflag = 'DEL' THEN tgt_inst_row_key\n         ELSE src_inst_row_key END AS inst_row_key\n  FROM type_delta\n  WHERE deltaflag <> 'NOCHANGE'\n)\n \nINSERT INTO dab.ORDER_desc(\n  ORDER_key,\n  type_key,\n  seq_nbr,\n  eff_tmstp,\n  ver_tmstp,\n  row_st,\n  sta_tmstp,\n  end_tmstp,\n  val_str,\n  val_num,\n  uom,\n  data_key,\n  inst_key,\n  inst_row_key,\n  popln_tmstp\n)\n \nSELECT\n  focal_key AS ORDER_key,\n  type_key,\n  seq_nbr,\n  eff_tmstp,\n  ver_tmstp,\n  row_st,\n  CAST(sta_tmstp AS TIMESTAMP) AS sta_tmstp,\n  CAST(end_tmstp AS TIMESTAMP) AS end_tmstp,\n  val_str,\n  val_num,\n  uom,\n  CAST(3272468793414818894 AS BIGINT) AS data_key,\n  CAST(3183483525924477858 AS BIGINT) AS inst_key,\n  inst_row_key,\n  LOCALTIMESTAMP AS popln_tmstp\nFROM type_load\nWHERE focal_key IS NOT NULL\n  AND COALESCE(\n    CAST(sta_tmstp AS VARCHAR),\n    CAST(end_tmstp AS VARCHAR),\n    val_str,\n    CAST(val_num AS VARCHAR)\n  ) IS NOT NULL\n" pipeline=Type
